<span class="line" data-line="19" data-hl="true"><span style="--shiki-light:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">    """g(x,y) = Σ_m Σ_n f(x+m, y+n) h(m,n)"""</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="20"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    s </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> h.shape[</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">] </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">//</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> 2</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="21"><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> sum</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(f[s </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">+</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> m, s </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">+</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> n] </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">*</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> h[m </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">+</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> s, n </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">+</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> s]</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="22"><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">               for</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> m </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">in</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> range</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">s, s </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">+</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> 1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">for</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> n </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">in</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> range</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">s, s </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">+</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> 1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">))</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="23"></span>
<span class="line" data-line="24"><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">print</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(conv_at(f, h), corr_at(f, h))                  </span><span style="--shiki-light:#6A737D;--shiki-dark:#6A737D"># -68 68</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="25"><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">print</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(corr_at(f, np.rot90(h, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">)))                    </span><span style="--shiki-light:#6A737D;--shiki-dark:#6A737D"># -68：卷积 = 核转 180° 后做相关</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="26"><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">print</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(ndimage.convolve(f, h, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">mode</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">'constant'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">)[</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">],  </span><span style="--shiki-light:#6A737D;--shiki-dark:#6A737D"># -68</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="27"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">      ndimage.correlate(f, h, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">mode</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">'constant'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">)[</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">]) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#6A737D;--shiki-dark:#6A737D"># 68</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="28"></span>
<span class="line" data-line="29"><span style="--shiki-light:#6A737D;--shiki-dark:#6A737D"># ---- pp.11–18：S. Seitz 的均值滤波 ----</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="30"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">F </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> np.zeros((</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">10</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">10</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">), </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">int</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">)</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="31"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">F[</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">7</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">8</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">] </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> 90</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="32"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">F[</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">5</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">4</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">] </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> 0</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="33"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">F[</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">8</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">] </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> 90</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="34"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">box </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> np.ones((</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">)) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> 9</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="35"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">G </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ndimage.correlate(F.astype(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">float</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">), box, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">mode</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">'constant'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">)[</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">]   </span><span style="--shiki-light:#6A737D;--shiki-dark:#6A737D"># 只留 8×8 的 valid 部分</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="36"><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">print</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(np.round(G).astype(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">int</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">))</span></span>

<span class="line" data-line="47"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">nb </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> np.array([[</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">124</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">126</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">127</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">], [</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">120</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">150</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">125</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">], [</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">115</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">119</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">123</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">]])</span></span>

<span class="line" data-line="50"><span style="--shiki-light:#6A737D;--shiki-dark:#6A737D"># ---- p.22：可分离 ----</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="51"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">g1 </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> np.array([</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">4</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">6</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">4</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">]) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> 16</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="52"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">K </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> np.outer(g1, g1)                                  </span><span style="--shiki-light:#6A737D;--shiki-dark:#6A737D"># 5×5，等于 (1/256)[...]</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="53"><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">print</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">((K </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">*</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> 256</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">).astype(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">int</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">)[</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">])                       </span><span style="--shiki-light:#6A737D;--shiki-dark:#6A737D"># [ 6 24 36 24  6]</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="54"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">img </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> np.random.default_rng(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">).random((</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">64</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">64</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">))</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="55"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">two_d </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ndimage.convolve(img, K, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">mode</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">'nearest'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">)</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="56"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">sep </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ndimage.convolve1d(ndimage.convolve1d(img, g1, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">axis</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">mode</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">'nearest'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">), g1, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">axis</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">mode</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">'nearest'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">)</span></span>

<span class="line" data-line="5"><span style="--shiki-light:#6A737D;--shiki-dark:#6A737D"># 一幅干净的测试图：浅色背景上两个深色圆盘，取值 [0,1]</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="6"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">yy, xx </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> np.mgrid[</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">128</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">128</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">]</span></span>

<span class="line" data-line="9"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">clean[(xx </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> 88</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">**</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> 2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> +</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> (yy </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> 80</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">**</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> 2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> 26</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> **</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> 2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">] </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> 0.3</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="10"></span>
<span class="line" data-line="11"><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">def</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> psnr</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(a, b):</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="12"><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> 10</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> *</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> np.log10(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> /</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> np.mean((a </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> b) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">**</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> 2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">))</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="13"></span>
<span class="line" data-line="14"><span style="--shiki-light:#6A737D;--shiki-dark:#6A737D"># 椒盐噪声：每个像素以概率 p 变成 0 或 1</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="15"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">p </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> 0.08</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="16"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">noisy </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> clean.copy()</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="17"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">hit </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> rng.random(clean.shape) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> p</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="18"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">noisy[hit] </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> rng.integers(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, hit.sum())</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="19"></span>
<span class="line" data-line="20"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">mean3 </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ndimage.uniform_filter(noisy, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">size</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">mode</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">'nearest'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">)</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="21"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">med3 </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ndimage.median_filter(noisy, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">size</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">mode</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">'nearest'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">)</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="22"><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">print</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">f</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">"噪声图 </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">{</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">psnr(noisy, clean)</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:.1f</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF"> dB   3×3 均值 </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">{</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">psnr(mean3, clean)</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:.1f</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF"> dB   3×3 中值 </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">{</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">psnr(med3, clean)</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:.1f</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF"> dB"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">)</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="23"><span style="--shiki-light:#6A737D;--shiki-dark:#6A737D"># 噪声图 15.8 dB   3×3 均值 23.3 dB   3×3 中值 33.9 dB</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="24"></span>
<span class="line" data-line="25"><span style="--shiki-light:#6A737D;--shiki-dark:#6A737D"># 拉普拉斯：[0 1 0; 1 -4 1; 0 1 0]</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="26"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">lap </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> np.array([[</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">], [</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">4</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">], [</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">]])</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="27"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">L_raw </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ndimage.convolve(noisy, lap, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">mode</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">'nearest'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">)                               </span><span style="--shiki-light:#6A737D;--shiki-dark:#6A737D"># 有噪声直接做</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="28"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">L_log </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ndimage.convolve(ndimage.gaussian_filter(clean, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">1.5</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">), lap, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">mode</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">'nearest'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">)  </span><span style="--shiki-light:#6A737D;--shiki-dark:#6A737D"># 先平滑再拉普拉斯</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="29"><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">print</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(np.abs(L_raw).max().round(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">), np.abs(L_log).max().round(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">))                  </span><span style="--shiki-light:#6A737D;--shiki-dark:#6A737D"># 3.82 0.065</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="30"></span>
<span class="line" data-line="31"><span style="--shiki-light:#6A737D;--shiki-dark:#6A737D"># 锐化：sharpened = f + α (f − G*f)</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="32"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">alpha </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> 1.0</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="33"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">blur </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ndimage.gaussian_filter(clean, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">sigma</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">1.0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">mode</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">'nearest'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">)</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="34"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">sharp </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> clean </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">+</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> alpha </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">*</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> (clean </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> blur)</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="35"><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">print</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(sharp.min().round(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">), sharp.max().round(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">))      </span><span style="--shiki-light:#6A737D;--shiki-dark:#6A737D"># 0.075 1.039：边缘两侧过冲，低于 0.3、高于 0.82</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="36"></span>
<span class="line" data-line="37"><span style="--shiki-light:#6A737D;--shiki-dark:#6A737D"># PyTorch 的 conv2d 实际做的是相关：p.7 的数据给出 +68</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="38"><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> torch</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="39"><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> torch.nn.functional </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">as</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> F</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="40"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">f </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> torch.tensor([[</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">43</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">., </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">12</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">., </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">61</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">.], [</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">44</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">., </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">45</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">., </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">60</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">.], [</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">43</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">., </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">50</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">., </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">61</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">.]]).view(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">)</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="41"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">h </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span <span class="line" data-line="42"><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">print</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(F.conv2d(f, h).item(), F.conv2d(f, torch.flip(h, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">dims</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">[</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">])).item())   </span><span style="--shiki-light:#6A737D;--shiki-dark:#6A737D"># 68.0 -68.0</span></span></code></pre>a7:["$","figure",null,{"className":"my-5","children":[["$","div",null,{"className":"overflow-hidden rounded-md border border-line bg-surface","children":[["$","div",null,{"className":"flex items-center gap-2 border-b border-line bg-surface-2 px-3.5 py-1.5","children":["$","span",null,{"className":"font-mono text-[11px] text-muted","children":"denoise_sharpen_laplacian.py"}]}],["$","div",null,{"className":"vml-code overflow-x-auto px-1 py-3 text-[12.5px] leading-[1.7]","dangerouslySetInnerHTML":{"__html":"$24a"}}]]}],"$undefined"]}]

<span class="line" data-line="2"><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> numpy </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">as</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> np</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="3"></span>
<span class="line" data-line="4"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">img </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> cv2.imread(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">'coins.png'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, cv2.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">IMREAD_GRAYSCALE</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">).astype(np.float32)</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="5"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">h </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> np.array([[</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">], [</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">], [</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">]], np.float32)</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="6"></span>
<span class="line" data-line="7"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">corr </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> cv2.filter2D(img, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, h)                     </span><span style="--shiki-light:#6A737D;--shiki-dark:#6A737D"># filter2D 做的是相关（ddepth=-1 表示输出类型同输入）</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="8"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">conv </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> cv2.filter2D(img, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, cv2.flip(h, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">))       </span><span style="--shiki-light:#6A737D;--shiki-dark:#6A737D"># 核先转 180°（flipCode=-1），才是卷积</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="9"><span style="--shiki-light:#6A737D;--shiki-dark:#6A737D"># 注意：filter2D 默认边界是 BORDER_REFLECT_101，不是补零</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="10"></span>
<span class="line" data-line="11"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">mean3 </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> cv2.blur(img, (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">))                       </span><span style="--shiki-light:#6A737D;--shiki-dark:#6A737D"># 3×3 均值</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="12"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">gauss </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> cv2.GaussianBlur(img, (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">), </span><span style="--shiki-light:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">sigmaX</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">1.5</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">)   </span><span style="--shiki-light:#6A737D;--shiki-dark:#6A737D"># ksize=(0,0) 时由 σ 自动决定核大小</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="13"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">med3 </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> cv2.medianBlur(img.astype(np.uint8), </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">)      </span><span style="--shiki-light:#6A737D;--shiki-dark:#6A737D"># 中值；ksize 必须是大于 1 的奇数</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="14"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">lap </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> cv2.Laplacian(img, cv2.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">CV_32F</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">ksize</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">)       </span><span style="--shiki-light:#6A737D;--shiki-dark:#6A737D"># ksize=1 就是 [0 1 0; 1 -4 1; 0 1 0]</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="15"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">sharp </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> cv2.addWeighted(img, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> +</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> 1.0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, gauss, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">1.0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">)   </span><span style="--shiki-light:#6A737D;--shiki-dark:#6A737D"># f + α(f − G*f)，α = 1</span></span></code></pre>a8:["$","figure",null,{"className":"my-5","children":[["$","div",null,{"className":"overflow-hidden rounded-md border border-line bg-surface","children":[["$","div",null,{"className":"flex items-center gap-2 border-b border-line bg-surface-2 px-3.5 py-1.5","children":["$","span",null,{"className":"font-mono text-[11px] text-muted","children":"opencv_filters.py"}]}],["$","div",null,{"className":"vml-code overflow-x-auto px-1 py-3 text-[12.5px] leading-[1.7]","dangerouslySetInnerHTML":{"__html":"$24b"}}]]}],"$undefined"]}]
